6. PosMean (Posterior Mean): Purely exploitative, would worsen the over-exploitation.
7. PosSTD (Posterior Standard Deviation): Has been tried recently (N=30-40) and failed to break the stagnation.
8. TS (Thompson Sampling): Has been tried (N=12, 19) and was successful earlier, but we've been using it recently (N=19) and it didn't break the stagnation.

10. qPES (Predictive Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=23) and failed.
11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=21) and failed.
12. qJES (Joint Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=24) and failed.

1. The optimization has been stuck at f_min = -0.916 for 32 iterations (N=15 to N=47).
2. The shortest distance (0.624813) is greater than the smallest lengthscale (0.590), indicating we've moved away from over-exploitation.
3. The lengthscales have decreased from the previous state (mean 137.763 at N=46 to 57.942 at N=47), suggesting the model is now more sensitive to changes in the input space.
4. The outputscale has decreased (3.5567 vs. 3.6792 at N=46), indicating slightly less variation in the function values.

Let's review the available acquisition functions and their suitability:
1. PI (Probability of Improvement): Too conservative, likely won't break the stagnation.
2. LogPI (Log Probability of Improvement): Similar to PI, too conservative.
3. EI (Expected Improvement): Has been tried before (N=10, 16) and failed to break the stagnation.
4. LogEI (Log Expected Improvement): Has been tried (N=25) and failed.
5. UCB (Upper Confidence Bound): Has been tried (N=11, 28) and failed to break the stagnation.
6. PosMean (Posterior Mean): Purely exploitative, would worsen the over-exploitation.
7. PosSTD (Posterior Standard Deviation): Has been tried recently (N=42-47) and failed to break the stagnation.
8. TS (Thompson Sampling): Has been tried (N=12, 19) and was successful earlier, but we've been using it recently (N=19) and it didn't break the stagnation.
9. qKG (Knowledge Gradient): Has been tried (N=22) and failed.
10. qPES (Predictive Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=23) and failed.
11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=21) and failed.
12. qJES (Joint Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=24) and failed.

- We're no longer in a state of over-exploitation (shortest distance 0.624813 > smallest lengthscale 0.590)

Current state:
- N: 48 (48 points evaluated so far)
- Remaining iterations: 12
- D: 6 (6-dimensional search space)
- f_range: Range [-0.916, -0.637], Mean -0.867 (Std Dev 0.079)
- f_min: -0.916 (current best value)
- Shortest distance: 0.515807 (distance from last point to nearest other point)
- Lengthscales: Range [0.604, 53.546], Mean 16.799 (Std Dev 20.345)
- Outputscale: 3.8732
Key observations:
1. The optimization has been stuck at f_min = -0.916 for 33 iterations (N=15 to N=48).
2. The shortest distance (0.515807) is less than the smallest lengthscale (0.604), indicating we're back in a state of over-exploitation.
3. The lengthscales have decreased significantly from the previous state (mean 57.942 at N=47 to 16.799 at N=48), suggesting the model is now very sensitive to changes in the input space.
4. The outputscale has increased (3.8732 vs. 3.5567 at N=47), indicating increased variation in the function values.
5. We've been using PosSTD recently (N=42-48) to try to escape the local minimum, but it hasn't worked.
Let's review the available acquisition functions and their suitability:
1. PI (Probability of Improvement): Too conservative, likely won't break the stagnation.
2. LogPI (Log Probability of Improvement): Similar to PI, too conservative.
3. EI (Expected Improvement): Has been tried before (N=10, 16) and failed to break the stagnation.
4. LogEI (Log Expected Improvement): Has been tried (N=25) and failed.
5. UCB (Upper Confidence Bound): Has been tried (N=11, 28) and failed to break the stagnation.
6. PosMean (Posterior Mean): Purely exploitative, would worsen the over-exploitation.
7. PosSTD (Posterior Standard Deviation): Has been tried recently (N=42-48) and failed to break the stagnation.
8. TS (Thompson Sampling): Has been tried (N=12, 19) and was successful earlier, but we've been using it recently (N=19) and it didn't break the stagnation.
9. qKG (Knowledge Gradient): Has been tried (N=22) and failed.
10. qPES (Predictive Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=23) and failed.
11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=21) and failed.

11. qMES (Max-value Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=21) and failed.
12. qJES (Joint Entropy Search): Has been tried (N=24) and failed.
